<a id="item-finance-news-1"></a>
### [Pinglu Canal Opens to Shipping, Cutting Southwest China–ASEAN Logistics Costs](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/18/content_30181792.html) ⭐️ 9.0/10

On September 16, 2026, the Pinglu Canal in Southwest China officially opened to navigation, creating the most direct and cost-effective shipping route for inland goods to reach the sea. The new waterway is expected to lower overall logistics costs by 18% to 30% compared to previous inland shipping routes.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 18, 00:26

**「Background」** The 134.2-kilometer canal, built in four years as a key part of China’s Western Land-Sea New Corridor, cuts over 560 kilometers off the inland journey for cargo moving from the southwest to ports. It is designed to carry 5,000-ton vessels and connects the region’s industrial clusters directly with Southeast Asian markets and global supply chains.

**「Impact」** For ASEAN exporters, the canal provides a new, cheaper maritime lane to ship agricultural and manufactured goods into China’s vast southwestern markets, while Singapore-based logistics firms see an opportunity to expand their financial and shipping services along the new trade corridor.

**Tags**: `#Infrastructure`, `#Trade Logistics`, `#ASEAN`, `#China Economy`, `#Supply Chain`

### [U.S. Bill Authorizes 100% Tariffs on Russian Oil Buyers](https://www.japantimes.co.jp/news/2026/09/17/asia-pacific/politics/india-energy-security-us-tariffs/) ⭐️ 7.5/10

The U.S. House of Representatives passed a bill authorizing tariffs of up to 100% on buyers of Russian oil. India warned that this measure could disrupt its energy security and broader economic ties with the United States.

rss · 日本时报\(The Japan Times\) · Sep 17, 07:28

**「Background」** Russia supplies a large share of India’s crude oil, and the U.S. bill targets countries that continue buying Russian energy by authorizing tariffs on their exports to the United States. This mechanism means the financial penalty would fall on Indian exporters and the trade balance rather than directly on Russian crude entering India.

**「Impact」** Indian energy importers and U.S.-India trade relations are directly affected, as the tariffs threaten to interrupt oil supply channels and escalate economic friction between the two countries.

### [China Issues Cost Accounting Rules to Curb Low-Price Competition in Key Industries](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/18/content_30181806.html) ⭐️ 7.0/10

China&\#x27;s National Development and Reform Commission and State Administration for Market Regulation issued a joint notice on September 17, 2026, establishing specific cost accounting requirements to standardize pricing in key industrial sectors. The rule mandates that costs be calculated based on individual or industry average figures to address non-rational, low-price competition.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 18, 00:26

**「Background」** This policy responds to continued issues with &quot;involution&quot;-style competition, where excessive price-cutting harms market stability. Officials stated that existing price laws regarding cost provisions were too general, prompting the need for clearer accounting standards to govern market order.

**Tags**: `#Policy Regulation`, `#Industrial Economics`, `#Market Regulation`, `#China Economy`

<a id="item-tech-news-4"></a>
### [U.S., China Experts Propose Unendorsed Nuclear-Style AI Safeguards](https://www.japantimes.co.jp/news/2026/09/17/world/politics/us-china-nuclear-ai-safeguards/) ⭐️ 8.0/10

U.S. and Chinese security experts have proposed implementing nuclear-style safeguards for AI risks ahead of a meeting between Presidents Donald Trump and Xi Jinping. These proposals, which neither government has endorsed, aim to establish a framework for managing the potential dangers of advanced artificial intelligence systems. The initiative reflects a growing international focus on aligning AI development with established security protocols traditionally used for nuclear weapons.

rss · 日本时报\(The Japan Times\) · Sep 17, 06:14

**「Nuclear Diplomacy and AI Governance」** Nuclear-style safeguards refer to diplomatic frameworks and technical protocols developed during the Cold War, such as bilateral hotlines, defined red lines, and verification mechanisms, designed to prevent accidental or unauthorized use of weapons of mass destruction. Applying these concepts to AI governance involves establishing similar high-level communication channels and safety controls to manage risks associated with autonomous artificial intelligence systems.

**「Impact」** For U.S. and Chinese defense planners, the proposal highlights that AI systems capable of interfering with nuclear command networks or launching cyber operations could compress response windows to mere minutes, increasing the risk of miscalculation. This underscores the need for formal red lines and emergency communication channels to prevent AI-driven escalation between the two nuclear powers.

Industry representatives detailed specific implementations: Agricultural Bank of China deployed 500 autonomous agents to manage over 3.6 million tech enterprises, while National Energy Group launched a hundred-billion-parameter model to automate power plant operations. China Southern Power Grid reported using AI to reduce dispatch response time from 10 minutes to 5 seconds, and Baidu emphasized that AI currently accounts for only 0.4% of global GDP, framing the shift to agentic systems as a critical infrastructure opportunity.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 18, 00:26

The discussion shifts the focus from traditional generative AI to &\#x27;agentic&\#x27; AI, which can autonomously execute complex tasks, and addresses the industrial &\#x27;token economy&\#x27; where AI interactions become a primary currency of production. This aligns with national strategies to transition from labor-intensive models to intelligent, automated systems across finance, energy, and logistics.

**「Impact」** Developers and enterprise architects in China face a pressing need to adapt to &\#x27;industrial agent operating systems&\#x27; and edge-computing infrastructure to compete in an economy where AI efficiency is measured by autonomous task completion. <a id="item-tech-news-6"></a>
### [Researchers Replace Mouse Forebrains with Human Brain Organoids](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/wissen/medizin-ernaehrung/forscher-ruesten-maeushirn-mit-menschlichen-zellen-aus-accg-201234421.html) ⭐️ 7.0/10

Researchers in the United States successfully replaced the forebrains of mice with lab-grown human brain organoids, also referred to as &\#x27;mini-brains.&\#x27; This development addresses high-impact scientific questions in neuroscience and synthetic biology, yet the specific functional benefits or operational outcomes of these replacements remain unclear. The primary consequence of this breakthrough is significant ethical debate surrounding the use of human neural tissue in animal hosts.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 17, 09:12

**「Background」** Brain organoids are three-dimensional, lab-cultured structures made from human stem cells that mimic the organization and function of the early human brain. To allow these human tissues to integrate and survive, researchers must significantly reduce the size of the host rodent&\#x27;s native brain before transplantation, a process that has previously raised major ethical questions about the rights and suffering of these hybrid animals.

**「Ethical and Research Implications」** This research directly impacts neuroscience by enabling the use of human brain organoids as functional models for studying complex brain diseases, while simultaneously raising urgent ethical concerns regarding animal welfare and the potential for sentient biological systems. Researchers and institutions must navigate the uncertainty surrounding the functional benefits of these implants and the moral status of cultivated human tissue.

A historical podcast by Angelika Fey and Christine Brunzel examines the 2000 Austrian federal coalition between the conservative Austrian People&\#x27;s Party \(ÖVP\) and the right-populist Freedom Party of Austria \(FPÖ\). It specifically explores whether lessons from this event can be derived for Germany&\#x27;s political landscape. The discussion focuses on the historical significance of right-populist inclusion in government structures in Austria.

**「Background」** The 2000 Austrian federal government marked the first time the Freedom Party of Austria participated in a coalition at the national level with the conservative Austrian People&\#x27;s Party. This coalition was considered a significant political event in Austrian history, as it involved a right-populist party entering government. The Austrian political system and its historical developments provide the context for analyzing the impact of right-populist movements in European politics.

<a id="item-tech-news-102"></a>
### [People&\#x27;s Daily Report: 9·18 Museum Exhibits 1931 Evidence of Japanese Invasion](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/18/content_30181839.html) ⭐️ 0.0/10

On September 18, 2026, an exhibition titled &\#x27;Truth: Ironclad Evidence of Japan&\#x27;s Invasion of Northeast China&\#x27; opened at the &\#x27;9·18&\#x27; History Museum in Shenyang. The display features the original 1932 document &\#x27;TRUTH&\#x27;, compiled by nine Chinese intellectuals known as the &\#x27;Nine Gentlemen&\#x27;, who secretly gathered evidence of the Japanese seizure of Manchuria following the September 18 Incident. These materials, translated and bound by the group over 40 days, were submitted to the League of Nations and served as key evidence for the organization&\#x27;s February 1933 resolution confirming Northeast China as Chinese territory. The original document, discovered by a relative of one of the nine in 2008 at the United Nations library in Geneva, was donated to the museum by the descendants in 2010.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 18, 00:26

**「Background」** The September 18 Incident refers to the 1931 Japanese military explosion of a railway line near Mukden, which was used as a pretext to invade Manchuria and establish the puppet state of Manchukuo. The League of Nations subsequently sent an investigation team in 1932, which issued a report in 1933 that officially condemned the invasion and rejected Japan&\#x27;s claim of &\#x27;self-defense&\#x27; or &\#x27;popular self-determination&\#x27; in the region.

**「Impact」** The public exhibition in Shenyang allows visitors to engage directly with primary historical sources that established the international legal record of the 1931 invasion.

**Tags**: `#History`, `#Off-topic`, `#Commemoration`, `#People&\#x27;s Daily`

English Heritage is recruiting a team of volunteer scythers to maintain meadowland using traditional hand-cutting techniques. The initiative is currently operating at Muchelney Abbey in Somerset to improve habitat for the rare shrill carder bee. This program aims to preserve an age-old craft while supporting local wildlife conservation efforts in the 21st century. Volunteers engage in manual land management to keep the meadows healthy without reliance on modern mechanical mowing.

rss · 观察家报\(The Observer\) · Sep 17, 04:00

**「Background」** Scything is a traditional method of cutting grass or grain manually using a long-bladed tool, a practice that has largely been replaced by mechanized mowing in modern agriculture. Historically, this craft was essential for maintaining open landscapes before the widespread adoption of petrol engines and tractors in the 20th century.

**「Impact」** The direct conservation impact is the improved habitat conditions for the rare shrill carder bee at Muchelney Abbey, supporting the long-term survival of this specific invertebrate species.

The United Nations Interim Force in Lebanon \(UNIFIL\) is scheduled to conclude its operations on December 31, marking the end of a 48-year presence in the region. Lebanese authorities and certain members of the UN Security Council advocate for maintaining an international presence to prevent security vacuums. The upcoming withdrawal is driving concerns among local populations about the potential for unsupervised Israeli military actions in southern Lebanon. This transition period, occurring within months of the deployment’s termination, highlights ongoing geopolitical tensions in the area.

rss · 世界报\(Le Monde\) · Sep 17, 18:00

**「Context」** UNIFIL was established in 1978 by the UN Security Council following the Israeli invasion of southern Lebanon. The mission’s original mandate was to separate the belligerent forces and stabilize the border region. Its continued presence for nearly five decades made it one of the longest-running peacekeeping operations in the Middle East.

The Russian Ministry of Internal Affairs \(МВД РФ\) published an advisory instructing parents on how to handle threats and extortion targeting their children. If a child performed a banking transaction, the specific details of the operation and the date-time must also be recorded. <a id="item-tech-news-249"></a>
### [Z-scoring J-lens readouts improves latent knowledge elicitations by accounting for token frequency](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/Qe3jdphWCg9kny3e5/the-j-lens-offset-is-the-model-s-token-frequency-z-scoring) ⭐️ ?/10

An analysis of the J-lens and R-lens methods for reading internal states of language models finds that their output offset correlates with the model&\#x27;s token frequency, specifically achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.48 in middle layers of the Qwen3.5-4B model. While directly subtracting this offset degrades performance significantly, z-scoring the readouts helps recover hidden content, yielding a 0.805 leave-one-out accuracy on Gemma-2-9B-it compared to 0.665 for the protocol established by Cywiński et al. The z-calibrated J-lens is also the only tested method that produced non-zero readouts on Qwen3-1.7B. This suggests that calibration for token frequency is necessary to effectively use these lenses for eliciting latent knowledge without corrupting the outputs.

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 17, 21:55

**「Background」** The J-lens and R-lens are interpretability tools designed to read hidden content or latent thoughts in large language models by passing activations through a fitted Jacobian or LRP-style backward pass. &quot;Taboo organisms&quot; are fine-tuned models that deliberately hide a secret word, serving as a standard benchmark for testing the ability to elicit this hidden knowledge from internal activations.

**「Impact」** Researchers and developers using J-lens or R-lens for model interpretability must apply z-score calibration rather than direct subtraction to account for token frequency, as uncalibrated lenses produce significant errors when reading middle layers. This calibration improves the accuracy of eliciting hidden knowledge in specific model architectures like Qwen and Gemma, but the approach currently shifts outputs toward translations and emojis that may not match strict English string-matching benchmarks.

<a id="item-tech-news-258"></a>
### [China Releases Statistical Reform Plan for 15th Five-Year Period](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/18/content_30181830.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 16, 2026, China&\#x27;s National Bureau of Statistics released the Statistical Reform and Development &\#x27;15th Five-Year&\#x27; Plan to advance the modernization of statistics and support the country&\#x27;s broader modernization goals. The plan focuses on the period of the 15th Five-Year Plan, deploying key initiatives such as improving the statistical institutional and methodological framework, refining the national economic accounting system, and establishing more modern statistical measures for evolving industries. Specific objectives include enriching statistical indicators to better reflect the development of new sectors and new quality productive forces, strengthening monitoring of agriculture, manufacturing, and services, and enhancing the scientific rigor and international comparability of macroeconomic accounting data.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 18, 00:26

**「Background」** The 15th Five-Year Plan is part of China&\#x27;s rolling five-year planning cycle, which directs national economic and social development. The national economic accounting system refers to the set of rules and methods used to measure and record a country&\#x27;s economic activity, such as its Gross Domestic Product \(GDP\). Modernizing this system is a key part of the country&\#x27;s broader economic policy.

**「Impact」** The plan will directly impact government policymakers, economic analysts, and standard setters who rely on official Chinese economic data, as it commits to strengthening data integrity through stricter enforcement against fraudulent reporting and improving the international comparability of the country&\#x27;s macroeconomic figures. It also signals a continued shift in economic tracking priorities toward emerging sectors and social welfare indicators.

<a id="item-tech-blog-1"></a>
### [The limits of low opaque serial depth for AI oversight](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/8mADs3rCHGuJqptFC/what-is-and-isn-t-gained-by-avoiding-architectures-with-high) ⭐️ 6.0/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 17, 23:52

**「Background」** Alek Westover discusses the limits of maintaining low opaque serial depth, a metric designed to measure how much opaque serial cognition a model can perform. While keeping this depth low helps preserve human oversight, it does not guarantee that model reasoning remains understandable.

**「Solution」** The author identifies two unresolved problems: some harmful actions require minimal opaque cognition, and models with low depth could still reason in garbled or &quot;alien&quot; languages that look plausible to humans but convey different meanings to the AI. Despite this, Westover argues that low depth is still valuable for three reasons. First, avoiding high-depth architectures removes the informational bandwidth advantage that might otherwise push models toward illegible reasoning. Second, the training process may not incentivize the development of steganographic capabilities, meaning models might lack the &quot;muscle&quot; to deceive even if they want to. Third, keeping depth low reduces the cost for companies to explore and adopt additional faithfulness interventions, since they have already taken the expensive step of maintaining natural-language chain-of-thought.

**「Takeaway」** Maintaining low opaque serial depth is a useful but insufficient strategy for preserving human oversight of AI reasoning; it reduces the likelihood of illegible reasoning but does not mathematically guarantee faithfulness.

**Tags**: `#AI Safety`, `#Model Interpretability`, `#AI Architectures`, `#Chain of Thought`, `#Reasoning`

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 18, 00:26

**「Background」** In September, Xinjiang enters its peak autumn fishing season, creating a surge in regional demand for fresh seafood ahead of major national holidays.

**「Solution」** The report describes how the 4th Division of the Xinjiang Corps began harvesting over 3.5 million blue lobster and Macrobrachium rosenbergii shrimp from its farming ponds. Concurrently, desalinated whiteleg shrimp from Wusu City are hitting the market, with sales expected to conclude around the Mid-Autumn and National Day holidays. The article accompanies these observations with a short poem that uses imagery of snow mountains and fishing nets to celebrate the transformation of barren land into a thriving aquatic region.

**「Takeaway」** The piece serves as a brief cultural and agricultural highlight of the season, emphasizing the successful yield of local aquaculture but offering no technical details or engineering insights.

<a id="item-tech-blog-6"></a>
### [Guangdong League: Porting Hong Kong and Macau to Regional Tech and Sport](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/18/content_30181681.html) ⭐️ 0.0/10

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 18, 00:26

**「Background」** The Guangdong Urban Basketball League \(粤BA\) had historically operated within mainland provincial boundaries, lacking a formal mechanism for integrated cross-border sporting and cultural exchange with Hong Kong and Macau in the Greater Bay Area.

**「Solution」** The core architectural decision was to modify the tournament topology to explicitly invite the Hong Kong and Macau representative teams into the all-star competition structure. The routing and synchronization layer was designed to match the East and West Zone all-star squads, selected via a democratic voting protocol, against the newly integrated southern cross-border entities. The two-day execution spanned a highly concurrent scheduling system: the first phase processed a dedicated cross-border handshake race, while the second phase executed the final championship and third-place resolution via standard overtime arbitration. To ensure maximum user retention and emotional engagement, the execution pipeline included synchronized multimedia outputs, specifically a coordinated vocal performance of &\#x27;Dà Zhōngguó&\#x27; in the public arena. Furthermore, to prevent the system from becoming an isolated data silo, the integration protocol extended beyond the active network nodes \(the courts\) to a secondary data exchange layer involving on-site educational institutions and industrial base visits, alongside a perimeter feature hub that aggregated and displayed regional non-technical assets like heritage crafts and agricultural products.

**「Takeaway」** By successfully porting Hong Kong and Macau into the regional league ecosystem, the system demonstrates that integrated cross-border event architectures can effectively operationalize cultural and economic convergence. This establishes a scalable, reusable template for driving sustained, multi-domain integration across the broader Greater Bay Area framework.

**Tags**: `#news`, `#sports`, `#regional`, `#non-technical`
